# Skills · J · part 26

785 entries, journal-of-applied-econometrics to joycraft-setup. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **journal-of-applied-econometrics**: Use when targeting Journal of Applied Econometrics (JAE) or deciding whether an applied-econometric manuscript that brings methods to a substantive economic question fits this venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, method-and-evidence bar, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-SocialScience-Journal-Skills/skills/journal-of-applied-econometrics) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **journal-of-banking-and-finance**: Use when targeting Journal of Banking and Finance (JBF) or deciding whether an applied banking / finance manuscript fits this venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, method-and-evidence bar, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-SocialScience-Journal-Skills/skills/journal-of-banking-and-finance) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **journal-of-beijing-sport-university**: Use when targeting 《北京体育大学学报》(Journal of Beijing Sport University) — a CSSCI comprehensive sport-science journal hosted by Beijing Sport University, China's flagship sport academy. Accepts the full breadth of sport science: exercise physiology/biomechanics/sports medicine, sports training & competitive sport, sport humanities & social science, and school PE. Best for substantial, well-evidenced studies across any sub-field; route narrowly applied/competitive pieces to 《中国体育科技》 and theory-defining work to 《体育科学》. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Sport-Science-Journal-Skills/skills/journal-of-beijing-sport-university) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **journal-of-biomedical-informatics-writing**: Journal-specific, verification-first guidance for preparing the MedVision manuscript for Elsevier's Journal of Biomedical Informatics (JBI). ([i-mTanvir/MediVision](https://github.com/i-mTanvir/MediVision/tree/HEAD/Journal%20Writing%20Reference/journal-of-biomedical-informatics-writing))
